[EVENT: TUR32116]
Date: 2023-10-28
Country: Turkey | Location: Bakirkoy
Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Turkey) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Anadolu Agency; Cumhuriyet; Haberler; Takvim [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 28 October 2023, around 1,500,000 people gathered for the rally organized by AKP on the land of the defunct Ataturk airport in Bakirkoy town, Istanbul, to express solidarity with Palestine and condemn ongoing Israeli attacks on Gaza. President Erdogan, and the leaders of MHP, BBP, DSP and HUDA PAR attended the event.

[EVENT: TUR32128]
Date: 2023-10-28
Country: Turkey | Location: Van
Type: Strategic developments > Change to group/activity | Disorder: Strategic developments
Actor1: Government of Turkey (2016-) [State forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Rudaw [Regional]

DESCRIPTION:
Security measures: On 28 October 2023, Van Governor's office issued a ban on all forms of marches, protests, press conferences, sit-ins and other political gatherings in the province for 2 days. Similar measures have been imposed in the province (and several other south-eastern provinces) for some years to maintain public order, presumably against political events that can be organized by the Kurdish movement.
